@255 FOREWORD
@72 The National Highway Planning & Design Circle has been publishing since a long time back
@72 the Schedule of Rates for different works of National Highways entrusted with the State
@72 PWD, Government of West Bengal by the Ministry of Road Transport & Highways (erstwhile
@72 MOST), Government of India.
@100 th @298 st
@72 The 6 @107 edition of the SOR was published on 1 @307 July, 2011 in printed form for the very last
@72 time. This remained operative upto 9th April, 2014 till publication of its 7th edition in e-
@517 th
@72 mode (uploaded in the PWD website) w.e.f. 10th July, 2014 for the very first time. The 8
@291 th
@72 edition of the SOR was published w.e.f. 16 @302 April, 2015 as per the directive of the then
@72 Principal Secretary to Government of West Bengal, PWD to the effect of updating of the
@207 th
@72 SOR @98 in @114 each @144 year. @176 The @201 5 @220 revision @265 of @282 the @305 Specifications @380 for Road @431 and @456 Bridge @494 Works
@72 published in August, 2013 by MORTH had also necessitated for updating of the SOR in the
@177 st
@72 year 2015-16.Since 1 @188 July,2017, the implementation of GST, has obviously demanded a
@72 change in SOR.
@72 It has been gathered, that, the Ministry of Road Transport & Highways, Government of
@72 India has also engaged a consultant for revision of the Standard Data Book for Analysis of
@113 st
@72 Rates (1 @123 revision, 2003). Once that comes out, it would be of great help as due to want
@72 of that, state PWD has updated the hire charges of T&P Machineries on the basis of WPI
@72 and the data collected by the field officers, over the years.
@72 Based on the input received from various reputed manufacturing houses /organisations,
@363 th
@72 related authorities and field officers of NH Wing, the 9 @375 edition of the SOR has been
@72 compiled by the officers of NH Planning & Design Circle following Specifications for Road
@179 th @518 st
@72 and Bridge Works (5 @191 revision, 2013) & Standard Data Book for Analysis of Rates (1
@72 revision, 2003) as well as GST rules.
@72 All users of this SOR are requested to feel free in suggesting anything for up gradation of
@72 the documents in future.
@72 Date: @106 30 @125 May 2019

@264 PREFACE
@482 th
@72 The Schedule of Rates for NH Works in the state of West Bengal was revised for 8 @494 times,
@360 th
@72 being the last one in the year 2015 for conforming it to the 5 @370 revision of the Specifications for
@72 Road & Bridge Works (published in August, 2013) as well as the Standard Data Book for Analysis
@130 st
@72 of @88 Rates @121 (1 @143 Revision @188 in @204 June, @237 2003).Due @291 to @308 non-requisition @386 of @402 any @425 modification @492 of @508 the
@72 prevailing SOR to the concerned authority, the same is still in operation.
@72 Since 1st July,2017, the implementation of GST, had obviously demanded a change in SOR,
@72 but, @98 non-reflection @172 of @188 necessity @238 of @254 revision @297 of @314 the @336 same @367 through @410 different @459 bidding @500 rates
@72 obtained all over the state of West Bengal, it was not updated. But, recently, it has been
@72 reported that for two/three ROB projects, quoted rates are far more than the sanctioned
@72 amount. Though that could be owing to different reasons altogether but, it has been decided
@72 by the competent authority of state PWD as well as the Ministry to have a general revision of
@72 the Schedule of Rates for accommodating the fluctuations in cost of Materials, Tools & Plants
@72 during @109 the @131 past @158 periods @199 vis-à-vis @244 the @266 significant @322 change @362 in @377 tax-regime @435 in @451 India @481 with @508 the
@72 introduction of GST.
@72 The basic cost of all materials has been established, on the basis of the input received from
@72 various reputed manufacturing houses /organisations, related authorities and field officers of
@72 NH Wing. State PWD has updated the hire charges of T&P Machineries on the basis of WPI and
@72 the data collected by the field officers, over the years.
@72 It has been gathered, that, the Ministry of Road Transport & Highways, Government of India
@447 nd
@72 has also engaged a consultant viz., Lea Associates South Asia Pvt. Limited for 2 @455 revision of the
@72 Standard Data Book for Analysis of Rates (1st revision, 2003). Once that comes out, it would be
@72 of great help for updating the rates established by the state PWD.
@72 As such the 9th edition of the NH Schedule of Rates @330 has been compiled by the officers of NH
@72 Planning & Design Circle following Specifications for Road and Bridge Works (5th revision,
@72 2013) & Standard Data Book for Analysis of Rates (1st revision, 2003) as well as GST rules.
@200 th
@72 The draft proposal of the 9 @210 edition of the SOR was placed by the Superintending Engineer, NH
@72 Planning & Design Circle & Member Secretary and Convener, before the NH Schedule Review
@148 th @167 th @190 th @211 th @232 th @254 th @284 st
@72 Committee on 6 @157 ,7 @173 & 8 @196 ,13 @218 ,14 @239 ,19 @263 & 21 @293 May, 2019. All the valued comments/suggestions
@72 of the committee members were incorporated and finally the committee approved the draft
@161 th @518 st
@72 proposal on the 29 @172 May, 2019 and resolved to publish the same as SOR for 2019-20 w.e.f. 1
@72 June, 2019.
@72 The uphill task of compilation of all the data collected from the renowned manufacturing
@72 houses, construction agencies, and construction officers of NH Wing was not simply possible,
@72 without the active help of Executive Engineers, Assistant Engineers attached with this Planning
@72 & Design Circle as well as the Engineer officers attached with o/o the Chief Engineer (National
@72 Highways). A special mention regarding the contribution of the Junior Engineers attached with
@72 the Planning & Design Circle as well as the NH Circle-I in preparation of the SOR 2019-20 must
@72 be made.
@72 Date: @106 30 May 2019

@239 GENERAL FEATURES
@105 1. @123 Back Ground
@104 The National Highway Wing has been publishing since a long time back the Schedule of Rates for
@104 different works of National Highways entrusted with the state PWD, Government of West Bengal by
@104 the Ministry of Road Transport & Highways (erstwhile MOST), Government of India.
@104 The 6th edition of the SOR was published on 1st July, 2011 in printed form for the very last time.
@104 This remained operative upto 9th April, 2014 till publication of its 7th edition in e-mode (uploaded
@104 in the PWD website) w.r.t. 10th July, 2014 for the very first time. The 8th edition of the SOR was
@104 published @149 w.e.f. @181 16th @205 April, @233 2015 @258 as @273 per @292 the @312 directive @354 of @369 the @388 then @413 Principal @455 Secretary @499 to
@104 Government of West Bengal, PWD to the effect of updating of the SOR in each year. The 5th
@104 revision of the Specifications for Road and Bridge Works published in August, 2013 by MORTH has
@104 also necessitated for updating of the SOR in the year 2015-16.
@107 Since 1st July,2017, the implementation of GST, has necessitated @381 a change in the SOR, but, non-
@104 reflection of necessity of revision of the same through different bidding rates obtained all over the
@104 state of West Bengal, it was not updated.
@104 It has been gathered, that, the Ministry of Road Transport & Highways, Government of India has
@104 also engaged a consultant for revision of the Standard Data Book for Analysis of Rates (1st revision,
@104 2003). Once that @180 comes out, this SOR would demand a further revision, but, pending that, state
@104 PWD has updated the hire charges of T&P Machineries on the basis of WPI and the data collected
@104 by the field officers, over the year
@105 2. @123 Scope
@104 The schedule covers both original works, repair and rehabilitation works including carriage of
@104 materials for National Highway within the jurisdiction of West Bengal.
@105 3. Validity
@104 This Schedule of Rates for NH works 2019-20 will come into force @383 from 01 June2019 superseding
@104 the Schedule of Rates for NH works: 2015-16, effective from 16th March 2015, along with its all
@104 addenda & corrigenda, without prejudice to the standing contracts of works.This schedule would
@104 subject to thorough revision once again to become compatible with the STANDARD DATA BOOK FOR
@104 ANALYSIS OF RATES, which is under revision by the consultant viz.,Lea Associates South Asia Pvt.
@104 Ltd.engaged by the Ministry of Road Transport & Highways.
@105 4. @123 Specification
@104 Nomenclature of items of work is brief, indicative and have been linked to the related sections of
@104 SPECIFICATION @170 FOR @192 ROAD @220 AND @242 BRIDGE @277 WORKS @311 (5TH @336 REVISION)-2013 @404 of @418 the @437 Ministry @474 of @488 Road
@104 Transport & Highways, Government of India. It is recommended that provisions of the publication
@104 SPECIFICATION FOR ROAD AND BRIDGE WORKS (5TH REVISION)-2013 shall form part of contract of
@104 works.
@105 5. Abbreviations
@104 Abbreviations of units as prescribed in STANDARD DATA BOOK FOR ANALYSIS OF RATES (1st Revision
@104 2003) of MOST have been adopted. Requisite listing has been included in this Publication.
@105 6. @120 New Item
@104 For any item not covered by the Schedule, if absolutely necessary on a work, the concerned
@104 Superintending Engineer will approve the rate with intimation to this office for records and
@104 reference.However; @191 this revision have @272 considered a good number of new @427 items of @468 work like
@104 Structural Steel works, Road Furniture items, White Topping etc.

@105 7. @119 Methodology
@133 With the introduction of GST,the Methodology of Analysis of Rates as obtained in STANDARD
@115 I)
@133 DATA BOOK FOR ANALYSIS OF RATES (1st Revision 2003) of MOST has been changed and now
@133 the rates shall include @226 a) Labour, T & P and Machinery ;
@133 b) materials without @224 GST @240 ; @249 c) overhead charges ; @346 d) contractor’s profit ;
@133 e) labour welfare cess.
@113 II) @133 To arrive for a particular item @266 the estimator has to consider the involvement of different
@133 components as applicable. These are @292 a) Labour , T & P and Machinery @445 ; @456 b) materials
@133 without GST ; c) Overhead charges @285 ; d) Contractor’s profit ;
@133 e) Building and other construction workers' @321 Welfare Cess @381 .
@133 NOTE: @164 Overhead charges (c) @ 8/10 % on (a + b) [ For overhead charges refer 9(II) of this
@133 chapter] Contractor’s profit (d) @10 % on @308 (a + b + c)
@133 Labour welfare cess (e) @ 1% on (a+b+c+d) shall be considered
@129 Therefore rate @197 = (a + b + c + d + e)/ unit
@104 III) @153 The overhead charges for road and bridge works include the following
@153 elements:
@138 1) @174 Site accommodation, setting of plan, access road, water supply, electricity and
@174 general site arrangements.
@138 2) @174 Office furniture, equipment and communications.
@138 3) @174 Expenditure @230 on @246 a) @261 Corporate @308 office @339 of @354 Contractor, @407 b) @422 Site @445 supervision, @500 c)
@174 Documentation and as-built drawings.
@138 4) @174 Mobilization/ demobilization of resources.
@138 5) @174 Labour camps with minimum amenities and transportation to work site.
@138 6) @174 Light @201 vehicles @241 for @259 site @281 supervision @333 including @376 administrative @441 and @463 managerial
@174 requirements.
@138 7)
@174 Laboratory equipment and quality control including field and laboratory testing.
@138 8) @174 Minor T & P and survey instruments and setting out works including verification of
@174 line, dimensions, trial pits and bore holes, where requires.
@138 9) @174 Watch and ward.
@135 10) @174 Traffic arrangement & management / Road safety during construction.
@135 11) @174 Expenditure on safeguarding environment.
@135 12) @174 Sundries.
@135 13) @174 Financing expenditure.
@135 14) @174 Work insurance/ compensation.
@127 Contractor’s profit @ 10% over all cost to be added uniformly.
@112 IV) @133 The cost of materials @224 shall include @277 a) Materials (basic cost without GST) @431 ,
@133 b) carriage, c) loading & unloading etc.
@112 V) @136 Labour rates are depicted in Annexure.
@112 VI) @133 Provision for Mate/ Supervisor intended for supervision of work and taking attendance of
@133 labourer has been included.
@111 VII) @133 The source of @199 all materials to be used @310 on the @344 project must @404 be tested @450 and expressly
@133 approved by the Engineer. Rates appearing in this schedule of rates have been worked out
@133 generally on the basis of current market rates received through different NH Circles for
@133 materials. As regards labour rates, those adopted in this schedule are @427 latest as laid down
@133 by Labour Department, Government of West Bengal.

@133 been included @195 in the analysis of @267 pre cast concrete PSC beams.
@112 IV) @133 Since @160 the @178 testing @212 of @225 materials @268 and @289 finished @326 items @354 of @367 work @392 is @404 covered @442 under overhead
@133 charges, the cost of such tests shall be deemed to be inclusive in the rate for related item
@133 and no separate payment is due to the contractors If the contractor intends to get the
@133 requisite tests carried out by an agency that agency is required to be acceptable to the
@133 Department and the contractor shall remain liable for bearing such cost of testing.
@113 V) @133 For innovative type of structures like cable stayed bridges, suspension bridges, arch
@133 bridges, bow string girder bridges, erected by innovative techniques where erection
@133 stage is as important as the construction of bridge components in terms of input of
@133 machinery, manpower and materials, special analysis is called for the items may not
@133 cover all components of bridge projects for all situations. There may be specialized
@133 items for specific cases, which need to be analyzed keeping in view the basic approach.
@105 11. @126 Guide Bund
@133 The items for the guide bund are excavation, embankment and protection works. The rates
@133 may be taken from the respective chapters.
@133 In case bridge construction works are to be done on wide and deep water channels in major
@133 rivers or in sea creeks etc., provision of floating barges etc. for taking the construction
@133 materials and equipments inside water shall also be made separately.
@133 Analysis for sinking of wells covers diameter from 6m to 12 m and twin D type of size 12m x
@133 6m. For other shapes like rectangular or any other size, the rates of sinking may be worked
@133 out on pro-rata basis.
@133 The lift for casting of concrete in well steining may be 2 to 2.5m restricting the free fall of
@133 concrete to 1.5m and concreting layer to 450mm.
@105 12. @126 Load test on piles
@133 Load tesing of piles @218 is incidental to work and is not required to be included in BOQ of
@133 contract @168 ,but,the same is required to be added in the estimate to assess cost of work.
@105 13. New features/items
@114 I) @133 Although the Standard Data Book for Analysis of Rates prescribes the unit of @421 carriage of different
@133 construction materials for variable leads as Tonne-Kilometre @396 , @403 the NH SOR of West Bengal
@133 uptil now was adopting the unit for carriage as M3/MT/Nos etc. @387 In this edition the unit for
@133 carriage has duly been modified as per the MORTH's Standard Data Book for Analysis of
@133 Rates.
@113 II) @133 Although @177 the @199 Standard @243 Data @269 Book @297 for @317 Analysis @357 of @373 Rates @403 prescribes @454 the @475 unit @499 of
@133 supply,fitting ,fixing of metallic bearing of Bridge structures as Tonne-Capacity @475 , @482 the NH
@133 SOR of West Bengal uptil now was adopting the unit for the same as KG/Tonne etc.,but without any
@133 clarity regarding the fact whether it relates to the self weight of the Bearing system or its load
@133 carrying capacity.In this edition the unit for different type of metallic bearings ( @433 Design vertical load
@133 carrying capacity in Metric Tonne @263 ) @271 has duly been modified as per the MORTH's Standard Data Book
@133 for @149 Analysis @183 of @196 Rates. @225 In @238 the @258 contract @299 BOQ @323 this @344 unit @367 may @390 be @406 in @420 Nos @441 also @463 for @481 better
@133 appreciation.

@112 III)
@133 usage rates of machine for hilly areas having @310 height more than as high as 2100 Mtr Recently Chief
